----- CLAUDE (2026-09-23 01:15:21) -----
All class/selector mappings match, each background is set exactly once, no duplicates or conflicts. The full picture:

hero(photo, unchanged) / about=`$color-surface` (`_about.scss:5`, unchanged) / projects=`$color-bg` (`_carousel.scss:13-15`, new) / work=`$color-surface` (`_modal.scss:11-12`, new) / video=`$color-bg` (`_video.scss:4`, changed from surface) / contact=`$color-surface` (`_footer.scss:4-5`, new) / footer=`#08090c` (`_footer.scss:16`, unchanged) — matches the ruling exactly, verified against the actual `class=` values in `src/index.html`.

`.work__card` correctly overrides to `$color-bg` (`_modal.scss:28`) since its mixin default (`_mixins.scss:29`) is `$color-surface` and it now sits on a surface stripe. `.about__col` already used `$color-bg` pre-existing, correctly left untouched. Carousel slide (`_carousel.scss:36`, surface) sits on projects (bg) — already contrasting, correctly untouched. Video wrapper has no fill, correctly untouched.

CTA cascade check: `.work__card i` (`_modal.scss:45-49`) and `.work__cta i` (`_modal.scss:70-75`) have equal specificity (class+element), but `.work__cta i` is declared later in source order, so it correctly wins and shrinks the arrow icon rather than inheriting the 2.5rem card-icon size. The hover/focus-visible nudge selector (`_modal.scss:55-58`) compiles to `.work__card:hover .work__cta i` — higher specificity, wins regardless of order, and the transition lives on the base `.work__cta i` rule so it animates correctly.

`_nav.scss:108-128` confines all size changes to `respond-to(small)` (max-width 600px); rules at ≥601px (mobile block at 768px, base, and `&--shrunk`) are untouched.

Scope: only `src/index.html` (6 spans), `_modal.scss`, `_nav.scss`, and the section-background lines in `_carousel.scss`, `_video.scss`, `_footer.scss` changed — matches the allowed-file list exactly.

### Spec Compliance
All three rulings fully satisfied: (1) alternating backgrounds match exactly, with the one required surface→bg override (`.work__card`) applied and no other element left invisible; (2) all 6 `work__card` buttons get the exact required `<span class="work__cta">` markup, with correct accent/small/semibold styling, hover/focus-visible arrow nudge, and a new `:focus-visible` outline on `.work__card`; (3) nav shrink is correctly scoped to `respond-to(small)` only, covering brand, links gap, and both normal/`--shrunk` link font sizes.

### Strengths
- Precise adherence to file scope — no edits outside the five allowed files/partials.
- The `.work__cta i` vs `.work__card i` cascade relies on correct understanding of SCSS nesting/source order rather than `!important` or extra specificity hacks — clean.
- Pre-existing correct cases (`.about__col`, carousel slide, video wrapper) were correctly left alone rather than "fixed" unnecessarily.

### Issues
No critical or important issues found.

#### Minor
- `_modal.scss:45-58` and `:61-76`: the small-arrow-icon override depends on `.work__cta i` being declared after `.work__card i` in source order (equal specificity, cascade order decides). This works correctly today, but it's a fragile arrangement — anyone reordering these blocks later would silently regress the icon size. Not required to fix for this task, just worth noting as a latent trap.
- Report claims computed-style/nav-fit browser verification but doesn't mention having visually checked the new `.work__cta` arrow rendering/nudge (only backgrounds and nav were explicitly verified in-browser). Given the CSS analysis above is correct, this is not a functional problem, just an unverified claim.

No element becomes invisible against its stripe, no rule leaks into ≥601px sizes, and no changes fall outside the allowed files. The only notes are a minor fragility (cascade-order dependency) and an unverified-but-correct visual claim in the report — neither blocks approval.

### Strengths
- Diff precisely matches the controller rulings on video (src path, no poster, `webpack.config.js` single-line change) — no scope creep.
- Selectors target the real DOM (`.about__intro`, `.footer__copy`, `.contact p`) rather than blindly pasting brief sample CSS, exactly as instructed.
- Consistent reuse of `$radius`, `$color-*` variables and `flex-center`/`respond-to` mixins — no repeated literals, follows `_hero.scss`/`_base.scss` conventions (2-space indent, `@use ... as *`, BEM nesting with `&__`).
- `.tags` correctly left unstyled in `_about.scss` since it's already handled generically in `_base.scss:84-95` — avoids duplicate/conflicting rules.

### Issues

#### Important
None identified with confidence from the diff and markup checked.

#### Minor
- `src/css/_footer.scss:26` — `.social-icons a` transition lists `color 0.2s, transform 0.2s` as two separate properties; other partials (e.g. `_about.scss:14`, brief convention) use a single `transition: transform 0.3s ease` per property being animated, which is fine, but here the two durations (0.2s vs the about card's 0.3s) are a slightly inconsistent hover-timing convention across partials. Cosmetic only, not worth blocking.
- `src/css/_about.scss:31` — `margin-bottom: 1.25rem` on `.about__col p` is a magic number not tied to a spacing variable/scale; the codebase doesn't define spacing variables so this is consistent with existing practice (e.g. `2rem`, `3rem` used ad hoc elsewhere), so not flagged as Important, but worth noting as an opportunity if a spacing scale is ever introduced.
- No `@media` fallback narrower than mobile for `.social-icons` gap/icon size — at very narrow viewports (not in the 5 required resolutions) three icons at `1.6rem` with `1.5rem` gap remain comfortably within width, so this is not a real risk for the graded resolutions, listed only for completeness.
